Collection of acoustic data (detailed description):

Outline steps which will be taken to collect acoustic data to provide information on the distribution and abundance of krill (Euphausia superba) and other pelagic species such as myctophids and salps (SC-CAMLR-XXX, paragraph 2.10).

### GUIDELINES FOR ESTIMATING THE GREEN WEIGHT OF KRILL CAUGHT

| Method | Equation (kg) | Parameter | Parameter | Parameter | Parameter |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Method | Equation (kg) | Description | Type | Estimation method | Unit |
| Holding tank volume | W*L*H*ρ*1 000 | W = tank width | Constant | Measure at the start of fishing | m |
| Holding tank volume | W*L*H*ρ*1 000 | L = tank length | Constant | Measure at the start of fishing | m |
| Holding tank volume | W*L*H*ρ*1 000 | ρ = volume-to-mass conversion factor | Variable | Volume-to-mass conversion | kg/litre |
| Holding tank volume | W*L*H*ρ*1 000 | H = depth of krill in tank | Haul-specific | Direct observation | m |
| Flow meter[^ntr5-L_202500202EN.018801-E0005] | V*Fkrill*ρ | V = volume of krill and water combined | Haul[^ntr5-L_202500202EN.018801-E0005]-specific | Direct observation | litre |
| Flow meter[^ntr5-L_202500202EN.018801-E0005] | V*Fkrill*ρ | Fkrill = fraction of krill in the sample | Haul[^ntr5-L_202500202EN.018801-E0005]-specific | Flow meter volume correction | — |
| Flow meter[^ntr5-L_202500202EN.018801-E0005] | V*Fkrill*ρ | ρ = volume-to-mass conversion factor | Variable | Volume-to-mass conversion | kg/litre |
| Flow meter[^ntr6-L_202500202EN.018801-E0006] | (V*ρ)–M | V = volume of krill paste | Haul[^ntr5-L_202500202EN.018801-E0005]-specific | Direct observation | litre |
| Flow meter[^ntr6-L_202500202EN.018801-E0006] | (V*ρ)–M | M = amount of water added to the process, converted to mass | Haul[^ntr5-L_202500202EN.018801-E0005]-specific | Direct observation | kg |
| Flow meter[^ntr6-L_202500202EN.018801-E0006] | (V*ρ)–M | ρ = density of krill paste | Variable | Direct observation | kg/litre |
| Flow scale | M*(1–F) | M = mass of krill and water combined | Haul[^ntr6-L_202500202EN.018801-E0006]-specific | Direct observation | kg |
| Flow scale | M*(1–F) | F = fraction of water in the sample | Variable | Flow scale mass correction | — |
| Plate tray | (M–Mtray)*N | Mtray = mass of empty tray | Constant | Direct observation prior to fishing | kg |
| Plate tray | (M–Mtray)*N | M = mean mass of krill and tray combined | Variable | Direct observation, prior to freezing with water drained | kg |
| Plate tray | (M–Mtray)*N | N = number of trays | Haul-specific | Direct observation | — |
| Meal conversion | Mmeal*MCF | Mmeal = mass of meal produced | Haul-specific | Direct observation | kg |
| Meal conversion | Mmeal*MCF | MCF = meal conversion factor | Variable | Meal to whole krill conversion | — |
| Cod-end volume | W*H*L*ρ*π/4*1 000 | W = cod-end width | Constant | Measure at the start of fishing | m |
| Cod-end volume | W*H*L*ρ*π/4*1 000 | H = cod-end height | Constant | Measure at the start of fishing | m |
| Cod-end volume | W*H*L*ρ*π/4*1 000 | ρ = volume-to-mass conversion factor | Variable | Volume-to-mass conversion | kg/litre |
| Cod-end volume | W*H*L*ρ*π/4*1 000 | L = cod-end length | Haul-specific | Direct observation | m |
| Other | Please specify |  |  |  |  |

[^ntr5-L_202500202EN.018801-E0005]: Individual haul when using a conventional trawl, or integrated over a 6-hour period when using the continuous fishing system.

[^ntr6-L_202500202EN.018801-E0006]: Individual haul when using a conventional trawl, or integrated over a 2-hour period when using the continuous fishing system.

### Observation steps and frequency

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| Holding tank volume | Holding tank volume |
| At the start of fishing | Measure the width and length of the holding tank (if the tank is not rectangular in shape, then additional measurements may be required; precision ±0,05  m) |
| Every month[^ntr7-L_202500202EN.018801-E0007] | Estimate the volume-to-mass conversion derived from the drained mass of krill in a known volume (e.g. 10 litres) taken from the holding tank |
| Every haul | Measure the depth of krill in the tank (if krill are held in the tank between hauls, then measure the difference in depth; precision ±0,1  m) |
| Every haul | Estimate the green weight of krill caught (using equation) |
| Flow meter[^ntr7-L_202500202EN.018801-E0007] | Flow meter[^ntr7-L_202500202EN.018801-E0007] |
| Prior to fishing | Ensure that the flow meter is measuring whole krill (i.e. prior to processing) |
| More than once per month[^ntr7-L_202500202EN.018801-E0007] | Estimate the volume-to-mass conversion (ρ) derived from the drained mass of krill in a known volume (e.g. 10 litres) taken from the flow meter |
| Every haul[^ntr8-L_202500202EN.018801-E0008] | Obtain a sample from the flow meter and: |
| Every haul[^ntr8-L_202500202EN.018801-E0008] | — measure the volume (e.g. 10 litres) of krill and water combined, |
| Every haul[^ntr8-L_202500202EN.018801-E0008] | — estimate the flow meter volume correction derived from the drained volume of krill |
| Every haul[^ntr8-L_202500202EN.018801-E0008] | Estimate the green weight of krill caught (using equation) |
| Flow meter[^ntr8-L_202500202EN.018801-E0008] | Flow meter[^ntr8-L_202500202EN.018801-E0008] |
| Prior to fishing | Ensure that both flow meters (one for the krill product and one for the water added) are calibrated (i.e. show the same, correct reading) |
| Every week[^ntr7-L_202500202EN.018801-E0007] | Estimate the density (ρ) of the krill product (ground krill paste) by measuring the mass of a known volume of krill product (e.g. 10 litres) taken from the corresponding flow meter |
| Every haul[^ntr8-L_202500202EN.018801-E0008] | Read both flow meters, and calculate the total volumes of the krill product (ground krill paste) and that of the water added; density of the water is assumed to be 1 kg/litre |
| Every haul[^ntr8-L_202500202EN.018801-E0008] | Estimate the green weight of krill caught (using equation) |
| Flow scale | Flow scale |
| Prior to fishing | Ensure that the flow scale is measuring whole krill (i.e. prior to processing) |
| Every haul[^ntr8-L_202500202EN.018801-E0008] | Obtain a sample from the flow scale and: |
| Every haul[^ntr8-L_202500202EN.018801-E0008] | — measure the mass of krill and water combined, |
| Every haul[^ntr8-L_202500202EN.018801-E0008] | — estimate the flow scale mass correction derived from the drained mass of krill |
| Every haul[^ntr8-L_202500202EN.018801-E0008] | Estimate the green weight of krill caught (using equation) |
| Plate tray | Plate tray |
| Prior to fishing | Measure the mass of the tray (if trays vary in design, then measure the mass of each type; precision ±0,1  kg) |
| Every haul | Measure the mass of krill and tray combined (precision ±0,1  kg) |
| Every haul | Count the number of trays used (if trays vary in design, then count the number of trays of each type) |
| Every haul | Estimate the green weight of krill caught (using equation) |
| Meal conversion | Meal conversion |
| Every month[^ntr7-L_202500202EN.018801-E0007] | Estimate the meal to whole krill conversion by processing 1 000 to 5 000  kg (drained mass) of whole krill |
| Every haul | Measure the mass of meal produced |
| Every haul | Estimate the green weight of krill caught (using equation) |
| Cod-end volume | Cod-end volume |
| At the start of fishing | Measure the width and height of the cod-end (precision ±0,1  m) |
| Every month[^ntr7-L_202500202EN.018801-E0007] | Estimate the volume-to-mass conversion derived from the drained mass of krill in a known volume (e.g. 10 litres) taken from the cod-end |
| Every haul | Measure the length of cod-end containing krill (precision ±0,1  m) |
| Every haul | Estimate the green weight of krill caught (using equation) |

[^ntr7-L_202500202EN.018801-E0007]: A new period will commence when the vessel moves to a new subarea or division.

[^ntr8-L_202500202EN.018801-E0008]: Individual haul when using a conventional trawl, or integrated over a 6-hour period when using the continuous fishing system.

## ANNEX VIII

## IOTC AREA OF COMPETENCE

1.

Maximum number of Union fishing vessels authorised to fish for tropical tunas in the IOTC Area of Competence

| Member State | Maximum number of vessels | Capacity (gross tonnage) |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Spain | 22 | 61 364 |
| France | 27 | 45 383 |
| Portugal | 5 | 1 627 |
| Italy | 1 | 2 137 |
| Union | 55 | 110 511 |

2.

Maximum number of Union fishing vessels authorised to fish for swordfish (*Xiphias gladius*) and albacore (*Thunnus alalunga*) in the IOTC Area of Competence

| Member State | Maximum number of vessels | Capacity (gross tonnage) |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Spain | 27 | 11 590 |
| France | 41[^ntr1-L_202500202EN.020101-E0001] | 7 882 |
| Portugal | 15 | 6 925 |
| Union | 83 | 26 397 |

[^ntr1-L_202500202EN.020101-E0001]: This number does not include vessels registered in Mayotte. It may be increased in the future in accordance with Mayotte’s fleet development plan.

3.

The vessels referred to in point 1 shall also be authorised to fish for swordfish and albacore in the IOTC Area of Competence.

4.

The vessels referred to in point 2 shall also be authorised to fish for tropical tunas in the IOTC Area of Competence.
